## Summary

The Python interface is implemented and wired to the existing C++ library through a pybind11 module. All seven mockup pages from `maquette/AiReview` are implemented with the dark-navy/blue/rounded-card styling.

**Implemented behavior**

- **Page 1/2** – live search by title or author, filter menu (Aucun / Emprunté / Non emprunté), 3-column clickable book grid with availability colors and borrower names.
- **Page 3** – borrowed-book detail with `Par : <name>` and **Rendre le livre**; returning switches to page 4.
- **Page 4** – available-book detail with a name field and **Emprunter le livre**; validates non-empty + existing user, then switches to page 3.
- **Page 5** – user grid (`N° USRxxx`) with back-to-books and add-user actions.
- **Page 6/7** – book/user creation with non-empty validation, duplicate ISBN/ID rejection, and the specified navigation.
- Data is auto-saved after every mutation and on window close; the real data is loaded on startup.

## Files

**Created**
- `agent/bindings.cpp` — pybind11 bindings for `Book`, `User`, `Library`, `FileManager` (returns copies, avoiding raw-pointer lifetime issues).
- `agent/requirements-ui.txt` — `pybind11`, `PyQt5`.
- `agent/ui/main.py` — entry point, `-d/--data-dir`, module-missing error dialog.
- `agent/ui/service.py` — validation/business layer, auto-save, Unicode-aware name and search handling.
- `agent/ui/theme.py` — palette and QSS.
- `agent/ui/widgets.py` — book cards, user cards, inputs, placeholder colors.
- `agent/ui/pages.py` — the five page widgets.
- `agent/ui/window.py` — `QMainWindow` + `QStackedWidget` router.

**Modified**
- `agent/CMakeLists.txt` — split into `bibliotheque_core` static lib, existing CLI, and `bibliotheque_py`; auto-discovers pip-installed pybind11; `POSITION_INDEPENDENT_CODE` enabled.
- `agent/book.cpp`, `agent/user.cpp` — strip trailing `\r` when parsing (see notes).
- `agent/.devcontainer/Dockerfile` — added `pybind11` to the pip install list.
- `agent/.gitignore` — ignores `__pycache__/` and `*.py[cod]`.
- `agent/README.md` — build/run instructions for the interface.

## Tests performed

- **Clean build** (`rm -rf build`, CMake configure + build): success, **no warnings** with `-Wall -Wextra -Wpedantic`.
- **CLI-only build** with `-DBUILD_PYTHON_BINDINGS=OFF`: success.
- **Binding tests**: 41 books / 25 users, 27 available / 14 borrowed; `find_book`, `find_user`, searches, checkout, return, save, reload; added a book and user and verified persistence.
- **Service tests**: empty fields, duplicate ISBN/ID, unknown borrower name, case/accent-insensitive lookup (`marie dubois`, `ÉMILIE PERRIN`), save/reload.
- **Qt offscreen UI test**: navigated every page, exercised the actual filter actions, search, return, borrow (error + success), add book, add user, and unknown-ISBN edge case; rendered screenshots for visual comparison with the mockups.
- **Real platform**: window creation and rendering verified with `QT_QPA_PLATFORM=wayland`.
- **CLI regression**: menu statistics still report 41/27/14/25.
- **Data safety**: `agent/data/books.txt` and `users.txt` checksums unchanged after all tests (tests used `/tmp` copies).

## Notes / deviations

- **CRLF loader fix (small, necessary):** `Book::fromFileFormat` and `User::fromFileFormat` did not strip the trailing `\r` from CRLF files. `borrower_id` became `"USR001\r"` and users' borrowed lists got a `"\r"` entry, which broke name display and return bookkeeping after reload. I added a minimal 6-line guard in each file; CLI and bindings both benefit.
- **Search:** the C++ search methods are bound, but the UI filters copies in Python with `casefold()` so accented French titles/authors (e.g. “Misérables”) match correctly.
- **Mockup examples vs data:** the UI shows the real dataset (`Marie Dubois`, `N° USR001`), not the mockup's illustrative names (`Léa T.`, `N° 1001`).
- **Display:** in this workspace `DISPLAY=1` is not a reachable X server, so plain launch tries `xcb` and aborts. Use `QT_QPA_PLATFORM=wayland` here (documented in the README).
- **Persistence:** the app writes LF through the existing `FileManager`; running it against the tracked `agent/data` will rewrite those files. Use a copy when experimenting.
- No automated test suite was committed because the repository has none; validation scripts were kept in `/tmp`.

## What you need to do

```bash
cd agent
# pybind11 is already installed in this environment; on a fresh container:
python3 -m pip install -r requirements-ui.txt

cmake -S . -B build
cmake --build build

# this workspace:
QT_QPA_PLATFORM=wayland python3 ui/main.py -d data
# normal devcontainer with working display:
python3 ui/main.py -d data
```

The new files are untracked, ready for you to review and commit: `bindings.cpp`, `requirements-ui.txt`, and `ui/`.
